# LanceDB Invocation Remote-Compatibility Audit

Status: current static audit for backlog 0076, refreshed by backlog 0092 and backlog 0096

This audit inventories production LanceDB/Lance API use under `src/lancedb_robotics/` and classifies whether each grouped call site is compatible with a LanceDB Enterprise `db://` remote DB, needs a capability check, or must use direct Lance object-store / Namespace data-plane access. The machine-readable source of truth is `docs/product/lancedb-invocation-remote-compatibility-audit.json`; this document carries the roll-up and the full invocation matrix.

Backlog 0128 turns the `namespace_or_object_store_direct_required` and `enterprise_remote_capability_check` rows outside the Enterprise training subsystem into runtime guards: `lancedb_robotics.capability_gates` maps the `direct_lance`, `blob`, `maintenance`, `index`, `versioning`, and `schema` families to a required `LakeCapabilities` flag and raises `BackendCapabilityError` (naming backend, family, and fallback) before the underlying `Table.to_lance()`/`LanceDataset` call when the backend does not advertise it. The Enterprise training loader keeps its own capability model (backlog 0069-0075/0116/0119) and is not double-gated.

## Scope And Method

- Included: `lancedb.connect`, `DBConnection` control calls, `Lake.table`, table reads/writes/search/index/version/schema calls, `Table.to_lance()`, `LanceDataset` scans/blob/maintenance/mutation calls, Namespace direct-access calls, `ColumnOrdering`, and `RRFReranker`.
- Excluded: tests, docs, fixture generators, pure `pyarrow` table/schema construction, plain Python collection calls, and provider/library version lookups that are not LanceDB or Lance calls.
- Grain: one row per source line/function containing one or more LanceDB/Lance calls; chained query builders are grouped into a single call-site row.
- Guard: `tests/test_lancedb_invocation_audit.py` rescans source and fails when the manifest is stale or the roll-up below no longer matches the manifest.

## Support Roll-Up
| Support class | Count | Meaning |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| `enterprise_remote_capability_check` | 81 | Likely needs explicit backend capability checks or live upstream validation. |
| `enterprise_remote_supported_now` | 792 | Expected to work over db:// now through LanceDB DB/Table/query APIs. |
| `namespace_or_object_store_direct_required` | 71 | Requires local/object-store LanceDataset access or pylance direct namespace; not plain db://. |
| `namespace_required` | 5 | Requires Lance REST Namespace/control-plane access. |
